# Graph Report - MythosMUD  (2026-10-13)

## Corpus Check
- 3480 files · ~3,525,757 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 59529 nodes · 115858 edges · 2385 communities (1739 shown, 646 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7393 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `c83fe032`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
